- **education-exam-prep**: Prépare un examen avec quiz de test, points clés et stratégie de révision. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur veut se tester ou préparer un examen spécifique. Se déclenche aussi avec "teste-moi", "quiz", "préparer l'examen", "QCM", "révision active", "simule un examen". Also triggers on "prepare for an exam", "practice quiz", "revision strategy". ([vantaflow/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/vantaflow/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/education-exam-prep) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **education-exam-prep**: Create or adjust a focused exam-prep plan inside a learning workflow workspace for upcoming tests. Use when the user mentions 考前, 测验, 考试, 冲刺, 目标分, or asks what the child should do before an exam based on existing learning records, Knowledge_Gaps, student work, and plans. ([Fuyera/education-learning-workflow](https://github.com/Fuyera/education-learning-workflow/tree/HEAD/80_System/skills/education-exam-prep) · ★1)
- **education-exam-prep**: Prépare un examen avec quiz de test, points clés et stratégie de révision. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur veut se tester ou préparer un examen spécifique. Se déclenche aussi avec "teste-moi", "quiz", "préparer l'examen", "QCM", "révision active", "simule un examen". ([carstenlysdal-alt/claudeproject](https://github.com/carstenlysdal-alt/claudeproject/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/education-exam-prep))
- **education-exercise**: Sub-skill do Education Agent para criar exercícios práticos isolados. Use when creating a single hands-on activity, coding challenge, or practical task with clear instructions and solution. Examples: "create an exercise on chain-of-thought prompting", "make a hands-on task for RAG", "build a coding challenge for n8n workflow", "design a practice activity for AI agents". Typically invoked by the education orchestrator skill, not directly. ([ppablobr/multi-agent-crew](https://github.com/ppablobr/multi-agent-crew/tree/HEAD/agents/education/skills/exercise))
- **education-expert**: Expert-level education technology, learning management systems, and ed-tech platforms. - **education-explanation**: Use when writing student-facing math explanations, hints, knowledge point summaries, common mistake analysis, or classroom-style content. ([xuyannan0321/math-ai-edu-site](https://github.com/xuyannan0321/math-ai-edu-site/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/education-explanation))
- **education-family-rules**: What an answer must contain when a learner asks about family history, pedigrees, inheritance patterns, carrier risk, or their own relatives — how to report the basis of an affected status, what to write when a pattern does not resolve, what to say instead of a personal risk figure, and how to handle two sources of evidence that disagree. ALSO LOAD THIS SKILL ON ITS OWN, WITHOUT THE EDUCATION SKILL, FOR ANY QUESTION INVOLVING A LEARNER'S RELATIVES, A PEDIGREE, A SEGREGATING CONDITION, CARRIER STATUS, OR RECORDING FAMILY HISTORY — "my father and my uncle both had it", "what does this pedigree s… ([mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public](https://github.com/mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-cutting/education-family-rules))
- **education-flashcard-generator**: Génère des flashcards de révision à partir d'un cours, d'un texte ou d'un sujet. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur veut mémoriser du contenu.
